### Community 421 - "🟠 HIGH (38 findings → 18 distinct issues)"
Cohesion: 0.11
Nodes (19): H10. Game completion awaits a Firestore write with no timeout — students frozen on game-over screen, H11. Students can reach the Thank-You page without the submit POST ever firing, H12. Axios auto-retries non-idempotent POSTs — duplicate registrations and retry storms, H13. Magic-link students cannot start: `studentDob` from redeem payload is never stored, H14. Mint-failure branches "fall back" to an auth path that was deleted, H15. Proctoring frontend grows unboundedly and drains CPU for the whole session, H16. Proctoring backend: blocking KEYS + full-payload reads every 5 min, retries run serially on the scheduler thread, multi-MB parses on request threads, H17. Dashboard snapshot can compute hundreds of MB on the request thread — heap-kill risk (+11 more)

Bulk-submit endpoints catch per-row errors inside one shared transaction — one bad row can roll back the whole upload after reporting success, L6. `processBatch` runs the full 6-phase pipeline for every mapping synchronously in one HTTP request, L7. Heartbeat endpoint trusts body-supplied IDs — any authenticated student can overwrite another's live position (+3 more)
